### Review fixes (before merging)
The `code-review` skill (high) on PR #10 found 10 issues; each was checked against the code and all 10 held:
- **Tokens could be sent elsewhere:** the consent fingerprint didn't cover where requests go, so someone with Edit could test (or save) settings pointing at their own host and receive the connected account's token. It now covers the base URL's origin and the allowed hosts (custom flows too).
- **App viewers couldn't connect their own account** on a per-user resource they have no grant on, though app queries would use it: `/api/oauth/start` and `oauthDisconnect` take `appId` (Use on an app whose live release queries the resource); the app banner passes it.
- **Switching shared ↔ per-user kept the other mode's tokens** (an old shared token came back when switching back): the token mode is in the fingerprint.
- **Waiters held pooled connections** while one process called a slow provider under a lock: `withRefreshLock` (try, sleep outside the transaction) for OAuth refreshes, custom sign-ins and connects.
- **A custom flow's forced refresh** compared with the row read at refresh time, not the variables the failing request used, so it could sign in again after someone else had: it now tracks what it handed out (like OAuth's last token).
- **Two connects at once** for the same owner could hit the unique constraint (a 500): connects take the owner's lock.
- **With custom auth the resource's default headers overrode the query's own**, and custom auth was special-cased in three places: one assembly for every auth type (above).
- **Every custom variable was scrubbed**, corrupting results containing a non-secret value: `secret: false` per variable (a checkbox in the editor).
- **Token endpoints that redirect** (a trailing slash) stopped working: same-origin redirects are followed.
Tests: 6 server tests (connecting through an app, testing settings that send elsewhere, base URL / allowed hosts / mode changes disconnecting, no lock waits in `pg_locks` during a slow refresh, a connect waiting for the owner's lock, a late refresh, non-secret variables), 2 connector tests (header precedence, token redirects); each fix mutation-checked. Correction: step 1 added 21 tests (16 in `oauth.test.ts`, 5 REST), not 27.

Bundles: main 425 kB (414 before M9).

### Engine fix, `25218f0` (session 12): a child ending while its parent pauses
The CI timeouts of "stops a workflow that calls itself after 10 levels" (4.3 s normally, past 30 s twice) were a missed wake-up, not slowness: a child's `wakeParents` that ran while the parent was still `running`, committed after the parent's `suspendRun` re-check had looked, was seen by neither, and the parent slept for its hour-long recheck. Now `finishRun`, `suspendRun` and `cancelRun` lock the parent's row first (`lockParents`, `for no key update`, ancestor before descendant); the reaper locks parents with `skip locked` and leaves a run whose parent is busy for its next pass. Regression test in `children-pg.test.ts`: a test-only deferred constraint trigger holds the child's commit open after its wake-up (failed before the fix: the parent an hour out). Step 6's 90 s limit for the test was dropped (it only hid the stall).

`e2e/m11-done-when.spec.ts` (with `E2E_GIT_REPO_DIR` for running it against another stack); the app editor stays mounted while main turns read-only after Protect (found by the spec: the Protect dialog vanished); `docker-compose.yml` passes `GIT_POLL_SECONDS`, `GIT_ALLOW_INSECURE_URLS` and `GITHUB_API_URL`; the test git server and fake GitHub take a listening address.

### Review fixes (session 12, PR #13 review; this commit)
Ten findings from the `code-review` skill, each checked against the code: (1) branch drafts and protect refused on the deploy branch (an Edit-only person could commit straight to main with the instance token, deployed unreviewed); (2, 3) changes and commit fetch the draft's base commit first (a new process's cache didn't have it: BAD_GATEWAY after a restart); (4) update from branch only at the revision it read (a concurrent save was overwritten); (5) a draft whose base commit is gone asks before being replaced; (6) a commit moves the base even when someone saved meanwhile (a false conflict with our own commit); (7) the deploy lease released only while held, and a request left at the round limit put back; (8) the rollback wording (above); (9) protecting again picks a free branch name, the Protect dialog leaving the default to the server; (10) one `liveOrDraft`. Tests: five in `git-branch-drafts.test.ts` (failed before the fixes), one in `git-protect.test.ts` (failed before).

## 9f. What M12 built (on `main`, PR #14)

### Plan, `c99a281`
`architecture.md` §2.7 (the M12 row, M13+), §2.14 (the design), this file's §14.

### Step 1 (this commit): the executor
- **The spike** (timeboxed, as planned): nsjail isn't in Debian (bookworm or trixie), so it's built from source (3.6, March 2026, pinned commit). In Docker Desktop: `--privileged` works; so does **`--cap-add SYS_ADMIN` plus Docker's seccomp profile with `pivot_root` added**, which is what we use (plus `apparmor=unconfined` for Linux hosts). Without SYS_ADMIN, no namespaces; with the default seccomp profile, `pivot_root` is refused; the cgroup is read-only until the entrypoint remounts it and delegates the controllers; a new `/proc` can't be mounted under Docker's masked one (the jail has none, so the loader needs `/etc/ld.so.cache`); kafel calls `umount2` `umount` (checked against both its amd64 and aarch64 tables). Timing: a jailed interpreter 12 ms, pandas' import 213 ms, a trivial block over HTTP 39 ms p50.
- `apps/code-executor`: `Dockerfile` (nsjail build stage; Python 3.12 slim; the virtualenv from `requirements.lock` with hashes), `seccomp.json`, `executor/server.py` (stdlib HTTP: `/run`, `/check`, `/health`, token, concurrency, cancel on hang-up, log framing and caps, outcome without a result → timeout/memory/exit code), `executor/runner.py` (in the jail: the code as a function body through the AST so line numbers and strings stay as written, `Obj` for attribute access, JSON conversion of pandas/numpy/datetime/Decimal/NaN/NaT, errors with the user's frames only, syntax checks), `executor/jail.cfg`, `executor/entrypoint.sh`.
- `@ow/code-executor/testing`: `startTestExecutor` (image tagged by a hash of its sources, built when missing; `PYTHON_EXECUTOR_TEST_URL`), `executorAvailable`, `EXECUTOR_DOCKER_FLAGS`.
- `test/executor.test.ts`, 23 tests against the container: the protocol and token; scope by attribute and key; conversions; empty code; prints in order with levels; a printed outcome isn't taken; syntax, runtime (trimmed traceback), serialization (16 MB) errors and `os._exit`; `/check` compiles without running; the jail (nobody, its own environment only, sees only `bin dev etc lib opt tmp usr`, can't write outside `/tmp`, no network even to the executor, no `unshare`/`ptrace`/`mount`, `fork` allowed); limits (an infinite loop at the timeout, 1 GB → memory and the next run fine, a fork bomb under 32, `/tmp` and file size, the log cap); a hung-up caller's jail killed; 8 runs 4 at a time; latency.
- **CI's first run** (x86-64, `f37c34d`): every execution failed with "exit code 255": the jail had `/lib` but not `/lib64`, where x86-64 binaries find their loader (arm64's is in `/lib`, so the Mac never saw it). Reproduced and checked without pushing: the image built for x86-64 under Docker Desktop's emulation, with a copy of the jail config without the seccomp policy (qemu can't install a guest's seccomp filter): without `/lib64` it fails the same way, with it 21 of the 23 tests pass (the namespace test needs the seccomp policy, and emulation is 10× slower); the x86-64 policy compiles. A jail that never starts is now an `internal` error with nsjail's reason (nsjail logs at `--quiet`, not `--really_quiet`), and the tests throw it instead of showing `ok: false`.
- Found while testing: a refused request's unread body was read as the next request on a kept-alive connection (`'{}POST'`); stdout and stderr on two pipes lost their order (now framed on one); pandas' `NaT` is a `datetime` (it came back as `"NaT"`).

## 9g. What M13 built (on `feat/m13-editor-polish`, PR #17)

### Plan, `2073d5d`
`docs/architecture.md` §2.7 (M13, M14, M15+ with AI actions last), §2.8 item 12, §2.15; this file (§1, "Taken in M13", §14, §15). Before it, on `main`: PR #16 (`c2cf787`), the CI timeout after #15's merge (a 30 s test timeout in the seven packages still on vitest's 5 s default: the runner was overloaded, the suite 80× slower than here) and a quadratic in `buildAppGraph` (a value read by 40,000 components: 2.6 s → 0.5 s).

### Step 1, `6b8a033`: history and workflow undo/redo
- `apps/web/src/lib/history.ts`: `record`, `undo`, `redo`, `seal` over snapshots (`{before, after, view, key, at}`, 100 at most). **Grouping is automatic**, not keyed at the call sites as planned: `changeKey(before, after)` compares the definitions by reference and names the plain values the edit replaced (`.blocks.1.config.code`); consecutive edits with the same key less than 1 s apart merge, and edits that change the shape (adding or removing blocks, edges, objects) never do. That covers every field (25 call sites) without touching them. `sameValue` (JSON equality, by reference first).
- **Found by the e2e:** some call sites rebuild the definition without changing it (a field reporting the value it has): 13 such edits while building a three-block workflow. They used to mark the draft dirty and save it again; they would have been invisible steps to undo. `update` now ignores an edit equal to what it replaces (`sameValue`): no step, no save.
- `apps/web/src/lib/shortcuts.ts`: `shortcutFor` (⌘ on a Mac, Ctrl elsewhere; Ctrl+Y redoes; nothing with Alt or while composing), `isTextTarget` (inputs, text areas, editable content, `.monaco-editor`: they keep the keys for their own undo), `dialogOpen`, `shortcutLabel`.
- The workflow store: `update` records, `undo`/`redo` (off while read-only or in conflict) put back the definition and the view (the open function and the selected block, when they still exist), `select`/`openGraph` seal the open step, `load` clears the history; `savedJson`, so an undo back to what's saved is `saved` (a save in flight finishes first, as in the app store); `epoch`, raised by undo and redo, keys the side panel's inspector, function settings and tabs.
- The editor: Undo and Redo in the toolbar (titles with the shortcut), ⌘Z / ⇧⌘Z / Ctrl+Y on the page while editable.
- **The Use-level fix:** people with Use load the main draft read-only (`mainReadOnly`: protected or Use), so edits are ignored and the stale check no longer reloads on every refetch; a read-only canvas doesn't drag, connect or delete.
- Tests: `history.test.ts` (10), `shortcuts.test.ts` (5), `store.test.ts` (7: exact definition back and forward, typing merged and split by a pause or a selection, the view restored or dropped, epoch and load, read-only and conflict, a save in flight, a no-op edit); `e2e/workflow-undo.spec.ts` (toolbar undo shows the block it undid, the code back as it was, keyboard redo and undo, ⌘Z inside Monaco is Monaco's own, what undo left is what reloads; Use: read-only, no drag, Delete or undo).

### Fix, `4926474`: a flaky check timeout
CI failed on step 1 in `@ow/sandbox` (a package it didn't touch): `python-client.test.ts` gave every test's client the 300 ms check timeout only "gives up on a check that doesn't answer" needs, and a busy runner took longer than that to get the fake executor's immediate answer back. The test client now defaults to 30 s.

### Step 2, `5b6411e` (+ `6426961`, the Python spec's clicks): copy, paste and duplicate blocks
- **The spike** (as planned): the browser's `copy` and `paste` events carry `clipboardData` under Playwright in Chrome, without granting clipboard permissions, from one tab to another. (CI's headless Chromium is checked by CI.)
- `packages/definition/src/paste.ts`:
  - `blockClipSchema`, the clipboard's JSON: `{ ow: 'workflow-blocks', v: 1, blocks, edges, positions, functions }`.
  - `copyBlocks`: the blocks, the edges between them, their positions, and the functions they call, followed through the functions those call. Never the start trigger.
  - `pasteBlocks`:
    - **Names:** fresh names (the stem and the next free number, never one of the pasted names, so renames never chain), given through `renameBlock` on a definition holding only the pasted blocks, so references between them follow and nothing else's do; a name a local would capture is passed over.
    - **What's left out:** the start trigger, a second error handler, and what a function can't hold (`notInFunction`, now exported). Edges to what was skipped go too.
    - **Functions:** added when missing, reused when the same (canonical JSON, layout aside), the target's kept when different, not added when a block has the name.
    - Connected after the selected block like Add; placed at `at` or moved by `offset`.
    - Lists references to blocks that weren't copied, and whether the target has a block of that name.
- `apps/web/src/lib/clipboard.ts`: `readClip` (JSON of one `ow` kind, parsed with its schema, at most 2 MB; anything else is left to the browser), `rememberCopy` / `copiedHere` (a hash of the last copied text in `localStorage`, shared by tabs), `textSelected`.
- The workflow editor:
  - `editor/clipboard.ts`: `copyText` (with the positions the canvas shows), `pasteText` (asks first when the text wasn't copied in this browser; to the right of the selected block, connected to it, or of the rightmost), `duplicateChosen` (⌘D, 40 px down and right, unconnected), `pasteSummary`.
  - `EditorShortcuts` handles the keys and the copy and paste events (copy works read-only too).
  - `PasteNotice` shows the summary of a paste (what was skipped, brought along, or still reads blocks that weren't copied).
- **Found by the e2e, twice:** "Maximum update depth exceeded". First, `onSelectionChange` as an inline arrow (React Flow calls it again for every new function) with an equality guard that still notified. Then React Flow and the store fought over `selected` after a click on a multi-selection (React Flow's `StoreUpdater`). Fixed by making the store the only source: React Flow's `select` changes go to the store and the nodes are built from it; `onSelectionChange` is gone.
- Tests: `test/paste.test.ts` (12, including 300 seeded random workflows: the target unchanged and first, the pasted names fresh and distinct, edges and references among the pasted blocks the copied ones renamed, no duplicate names; two mutation checks, both caught); `clipboard.test.ts` (3); `store.test.ts` (+1: the selection); `e2e/workflow-clipboard.spec.ts` (two blocks copied to another workflow after its block: fresh names, the reference and the function follow, the edges; undo and redo of the paste; ⌘D; a paste this browser didn't copy asks, and is pasted once accepted; plain text is left alone).

- `6426961`: `python.spec.ts` timed out in full local e2e runs (checked on step 1's code too, by stashing step 2): with the run panel open, `python1` sat under React Flow's minimap and every real click landed there. Its block clicks are now dispatched on the blocks (React Flow's own handler still selects them).

### Step 3, `536268c`: undo and redo in the app editor
- The app store, like the workflow store: `update` records each edit (edits equal to what they replace are ignored), `undo` / `redo` (off while read-only or in conflict) put back the definition and the view (the selected component, the open code item, the page; a component that's gone isn't selected, a code item that's gone gives way to the one open now or the first query), `select` / `openCodeItem` / `setUrl` end the open step, `load` clears the history; `epoch` keys the pages panel, the code panel and the inspector. Grid drags and resizes were already one `setPositions` each; app layouts count in the hash, and undo puts them back exactly.
- Shared with the workflow editor (which now uses them): `lib/editor-keys.ts` `useEditorKeys` (the keys and the copy and paste events, with the latest handlers through a ref, registered once), `components/undo-buttons.tsx`.
- In the app editor: Undo and Redo in the header, and the keys, while editing (not read-only, not in Preview, where the keys are the app's).
- Checked: people with only Use can't open the app editor (`apps.get` needs Edit), so it has no counterpart to step 1's Use-level fix.
- Tests: `apps/editor/store.test.ts` (5: back to the exact start and forward, typing merged per prop and split by another prop or a page, the view restored, what's gone dropped, no-op edits, read-only and conflict); `e2e/app-undo.spec.ts` (add two components, bind one, move one; each undo leaves the saved draft equal to the one before, exactly; keyboard redo back to the last; ⌘Z in a Monaco prop field is the field's; Preview has no undo).

### Step 4, `767222b`: copy, paste and duplicate in the app editor
- `packages/app-definition/src/paste.ts`:
  - **Two clip kinds:** `componentClipSchema` (`{ ow: 'app-components', v: 1, components, layout }`: components with everything inside them) and `codeClipSchema` (`{ ow: 'app-code', v: 1, queries, transformers, variables }`).
  - **Copying:** `copyComponents` (with `withDescendants`) and `copyCode`.
  - **Naming:** fresh names in the app's one namespace through `renameAppEntity` on a definition holding only what's pasted, so references, parents, handler targets and layout keys among the pasted items follow. As in workflows, renames never chain, and a name a local would capture is passed over.
  - **`pasteComponents`** into a target: into a container, else at the top on a page.
    - Roots go below what the target grid holds (`bottomOf`, now exported), keeping their offsets; what's inside them keeps its place.
    - Modals go at the top without a position.
    - Lists what the pasted components read that wasn't copied.
  - **`pasteCode`:** items that belonged to a page go on the page being edited, global ones stay global.
  - `runsByItself`: automatic resource queries and those that run on load.
- The app editor:
  - `focus` in the store: what was chosen last, a component (`select`) or a code item (`openCodeItem`), is what ⌘C and ⌘D act on.
  - `editor/clipboard.ts`:
    - Components paste where the palette adds one: into the selected container, else next to the selected component, on the page being edited. The selected component's own copy, and a duplicate, go next to it, not into it.
    - Code pastes into the code panel and opens.
    - Content this browser didn't copy asks first, **naming the queries that would run straight away** (the running draft follows edits).
    - The summary goes to the editor's notifications.
  - The keys through `useEditorKeys` (copy works read-only; not in Preview).
- `readClip`'s error no longer says "blocks" (it's shared).
- Tests:
  - `test/paste.test.ts` (7, including 300 seeded random apps: the app unchanged and first, the pasted names fresh and distinct, nesting and references among the pasted kept; two mutation checks, both caught).
  - `apps/editor/store.test.ts` (+1, `focus`).
  - `e2e/app-clipboard.spec.ts`:
    - A container with an input and a text copied into another app: names and the reference follow, nested.
    - Undo and redo of the paste.
    - ⌘D next to itself.
    - A query copied across reads the target's input, and says so.
    - Crafted code with an automatic query asks first, naming it.

### Step 5 (this commit): done-when, Compose check, docs
- `e2e/m13-done-when.spec.ts`, the done-when as written in §2.7:
  - **The workflow editor:** 50 edits, five kinds cycled ten times: add a code block after the last one, type its code, rename it, drag it, Arrange. ⌘Z fifty times leaves the saved draft equal to the start, layout included, **and the server's `draftHash` is the start's**. ⇧⌘Z fifty times gives the end again, hash and all. ⌘Z inside a code field is Monaco's.
  - **Copying with a function:** two blocks and the function they call copied into another workflow. Names, the reference, the edge and the function follow, and the copy runs.
  - **The app editor:** 48 edits, six kinds cycled eight times: add a Text, type its value, rename it, drag it, add a JS query, type its code. Undone to the exact start (definition and hash) and redone to the end.
  - **Copying a container** with an input and a text into another app: they stay nested, and typing in the pasted input shows in the pasted text in the running draft.
  - Locally both pass in 37 s.
- **Found while writing it:** the validator's `function_call` message says to call a function as `await name({ ... })`, but calls are positional, as the README and the editor's typings say (`await name(a, b)`). `sum({ values })` made `params.values` the whole object. Noted in §13, not changed in M13.
- **On Docker Compose (§10):** the same spec, passed.
- README: "Undo, copy and paste" (the keys and what each editor does).

## 9h. What the Optiply UI built (on `main`, PR #18 → `bb7df0f`)

The design is `architecture.md` §2.16. Seven steps, each a commit; screenshots of every main screen in light and dark after each step (a Playwright script against the e2e setup, kept in the session's scratchpad).

### Step 1, `76f6a3d`: tokens, fonts, dark mode
- Optiply's variables in `styles.css` (`:root`, `.dark`), Tailwind's tokens mapped onto them (`@theme inline`), `lib/theme.ts` and the pre-paint script in `index.html`, self-hosted Inter and JetBrains Mono (they were named but never loaded before), hard-coded colours to tokens in about 25 files.

### Step 2, `487af67`: components
- `ui.tsx` in Optiply's style: buttons (new `tertiary` default, `secondary` is now the blue tint), one input look, cards, `PageContainer`, the table and `TableFooter`, pill tabs, tags, the dialog, a new `Menu`, `PageHeader`, `SectionLabel`.

### Step 3, `a2d7ca4`: top bar, account menu, sign-in
- `app-shell.tsx`: the top bar; the account menu holds the theme switch and Sign out. `components/wordmark.tsx`. Sign-in restyled. New `e2e/theme.spec.ts`.

### Step 4, `67a3eb6`: pages
- Runs, Inbox and the audit log in page containers with footers and Optiply links; `ActionBar` for filters; folder headings as section labels; the Database page as two panels; titles on the resource and task pages.

### Step 5, `24aeced`: editors
- Monaco themes, React Flow's variables, block and resource-type colours from Optiply's tag scales; both editors as panels; the running app's page header.

### Step 6, `c00c6d8`: built apps and charts
- The app builder's renderers on the shared components (buttons, inputs, table with 40px rows, cards, dialog, pills), Optiply's chart palette in light and dark.
- **Tests adjusted to the new layout:** `m10-done-when`'s table is taller (40px rows; the virtualizer draws what's visible plus 10, and the test reads a whole page of 25); `editor.spec`'s rename step dispatches its block clicks (the minimap can cover a block when the run panel is open; seen in a trace, as `6426961` for the Python spec).

### Step 7 (this commit): check and docs
- **Contrast** in both themes (§2.16): warnings as text use `warning-strong`.
- **On Docker Compose (§10):** the app image rebuilt from step 6 and `api`/`worker` recreated (`--no-deps`; Postgres and its data untouched, no migrations in this work). Sign-in renders in light and dark with Inter loaded and no console or network errors.
- `architecture.md` §2.16, this handoff.

### Review fixes, `837eefd` (then merged as `bb7df0f`)
- The PR #18 review (the `code-review` skill) found 10 things. **The user chose:** built-app table rows **stay 40px** (existing apps show fewer rows in the same space); the canvas minimap becomes **smaller (140×96) with a hide button** in the zoom controls (`localStorage` `ow.minimap`), and the e2e workarounds go (`editor.spec` and `python.spec` click blocks for real again; 12/12 repeated runs).
- **One source for colours:** `lib/tokens.ts` `token()` reads Optiply's CSS variables of the theme in effect; Monaco's themes (`optiplyTheme`) and the charts' palette (`chart.tsx`) use it, and `chart-model.ts` takes a `ChartPalette` (no hex left outside `styles.css`).
- The chart crosshair's colour is a plugin option (it kept the first theme's after an in-place update); Monaco re-measures and charts redraw when web fonts finish loading.
- The `Menu` closes when focus moves out, sets `aria-controls` only while open, takes a `label`; `MenuGroup` (a fieldset) for the theme choices; the account details are `aria-hidden`.
- Shared instead of copied: `sectionLabelClass`, `LoadMoreFooter`. Unused API removed (`onThemeChange`, a button shadow, Dialog's `icon`, Badge's `outline` and two tones). "Waiting" status badges are orange.
- New e2e in `theme.spec.ts`: focus out closes the menu; hide the minimap, reload, show it again. Full e2e 38/38; CI green.

## 9i. What M14 built (on `main`, PR #20 → `bda0dbf`)

The design is `architecture.md` §2.17; decisions in §2 "Taken in M14"; the step-by-step record in §14. Eight steps, each split into parts that ended green (23 commits); a step was pushed once, when all its parts were in.

- **Library queries** (steps 1–3, `0be8d2a`…`5867acd`): `packages/app-definition/src/library.ts`; tables and migration 0017; `library_query` in grants, folders and audit; `libraryQueries.*` (`services/library-queries.ts`) with shared `nextReleaseVersion` and `runStoredQuery`; the `library` app query (pinned or `latest`), run by `runAppQuery` from the resolved release with parameters rendered lazily, Use needed to publish an app or run its draft, `usedBy` and refused deletes; the Library page and editor (`/library`, `/library/$slug`), and in the app editor a picker, version, parameter fields and the query read-only.
- **Modules** (steps 4–6, `12e319b`…`e88fa7e`): a module is an app definition with a `module` part (inputs, outputs as `$outputs.<name>`, events, `moduleEvent`) and no pages; tables and migration 0018, `modules.*` (`services/modules.ts`, shared `checkVouched`/`runDefinitionQuery`); at run time one child `AppRuntime` per instance with prefixed state, inputs in, outputs and events out (`packages/app-runtime`), `apps.open` returning the modules' releases and `apps.runQuery` taking `module` (CONFLICT "reload" when a `latest` instance's module moved on); the viewer's `ModuleRenderer` and `AppScope`; the Modules page, the module editor (the app editor in module mode with the Module panel) and, in the app editor, Add a module and the instance inspector.
- **Git** (step 7, `fc237c9`…`e8c31cc`): `library/<slug>/` and `modules/<slug>/` in `@ow/git-format` (round-trip property tests), migration 0019, `services/git/kinds.ts` (one table of per-kind behaviour), protection and branch drafts for all four kinds with guards in the library and module routers, deploy in the order library queries, modules, workflows, apps; the web's Git bar, protection and `?branch=` in the module and library editors.
- **Done-when** (step 8): `apps/web/e2e/m14-done-when.spec.ts` (`44d6358`): the three parts in one test; passed in the e2e suite (48) and on Docker Compose (§10).

## 9j. What M15 built (on the local branch `feat/m15-webhooks`, not pushed)

The design is `architecture.md` §2.18 (with "As built"); the step record in §14. The plan and seven steps, the larger ones split into parts that each ended green, plus two fixes, 15 commits on top of `b2e82a9` (PR #21's handoff). Checked locally before every commit: CI's GitHub Actions minutes had run out.

- **Definition** (step 1, `8f839e8`): `triggers.webhook` gets `auth` (apiKey, public, signature with a preset and a secret config var's name), `bodySchema`, `rateLimit`, `environments`, `idempotencyHeader`, all optional (no schema bump); `resolveWebhookSettings` holds every default; `WEBHOOK_PRESETS`; validator codes `invalid_webhook_auth`, `invalid_body_schema`, `invalid_webhook`; Ajv in the subpath `@ow/definition/body-schema`, passed to the validator as `checkBodySchema`.
- **Signatures and auth** (steps 2a `7f6b444`, 2b `761b857`): `services/webhook-signatures.ts` (five presets and custom over the raw bytes, timing-safe, timestamps both ways; secrets one per line for rotation); the body parsers keep bytes and parse after auth; the route's order (find, limit, method, environment, auth, body, run); public and signed webhooks; allowed environments; idempotency from a provider's delivery id; Slack's URL check; publish refuses a missing, non-secret or unset secret config var.
- **Limits, schemas, refused calls** (steps 3a `730538d`, 3b `26a6483`): migration 0020 (`webhook_rate_windows`, `webhook_rejections`); a fixed-window counter in Postgres (429 + Retry-After); retention for both; `ow_webhook_rejected_total{reason}`; `WebhookRejection` with reason codes; body schemas compiled per release (400 `invalid_body` with JSON Pointers, no run); every 4xx of a found webhook recorded (first 429 of a window only; 60 a minute per webhook at most); `workflows.webhookRejections` (Edit).
- **Multipart** (steps 4a `6c1d474`, 4b `792f84b`): webhooks parse `multipart/form-data` with busboy (files as base64 objects); REST queries send a `multipart` body (text parts, files from base64 or a webhook's file object) with the editor's Multipart option in the workflow inspector and the playground.
- **OpenAPI** (step 5, `18829a4`): `GET /api/v1/openapi.json` for a session or an API key, the webhooks the caller may call plus `GET /runs/{id}`, validated against OpenAPI 3.1 in tests.
- **Editor** (steps 6a `8d59e5f`, 6b `dd13e45`): the webhook panel's authentication, rate limit, environments, idempotency header, example call per auth and refused calls; the body schema in Monaco with drafts from the test input or a sample; `startTrigger.data` typed from the schema (`schemaType`); OpenAPI links.
- **Done-when** (step 7a, `823f071`): `apps/web/e2e/m15-done-when.spec.ts`; passed in the e2e suite (51) and on Docker Compose (§10).
- **Fixes on the way:** `341e019` (the server's bundler needs Ajv declared in apps/server: the server build had failed since step 1, unnoticed because only tests ran; the server build now runs every step) and `b06f4ff` (a Postgres query whose signal aborted while it waited for a connection ran anyway; found by a load-flaky test, a pre-existing bug from M1/M6).
- **Review fixes** (session 18, the `code-review` skill on `main...feat/m15-webhooks`: 10 found, all fixed in one commit): unknown URLs and API-key webhooks answer 401 before revealing anything to a caller without a valid key; signed and API-key webhooks count only authenticated calls toward their limit; public webhooks ignore idempotency keys (validator, editor and OpenAPI agree); every API key header is kept out of the run; the editor's schema types survive a broken `$ref` escape, self-references (an expansion budget) and unions starting with an object or tuple; `webhookEnvironments`/`signatureHeaderOf` in `@ow/definition` replace three copies; trigger rows store the webhook as written. Tests: server 372, definition 166, web unit 74, e2e 51. The Compose stack was rebuilt on the review fixes afterwards (§10).
- **After the review** (`41b7408`, from the user trying a REST resource on the stack): secret fields (resource secrets, the Git token) are text inputs masked with CSS (`SecretInput`), so Chrome stops offering to save them as passwords (it did for `type="password"` even with `autocomplete="new-password"`); a refused redirect says which host redirected where (without the target's query string). e2e 52.
- **New dependencies:** ajv 8.20.0 and ajv-formats 3.0.1 (MIT; already in the lockfile through Fastify), busboy 1.6.0 (MIT, with streamsearch), and dev-only @types/busboy and @seriousme/openapi-schema-validator 2.9.1 (MIT).
- **Totals:** server tests 368 (330 at the end of M14), connectors 147, definition 163, web unit 71, e2e 51 (48).

## 9k. What M16 built (on the local branch `feat/m16-multiplayer`, not pushed)

The design is `architecture.md` §2.19 (with "As built"); the step record in §14. The plan (`e0abdf1`, with M17 on the roadmap `0b3ee9b`) and eight steps, each split into parts that ended green and were committed on the user's word, stacked on `feat/m15-webhooks`. Checked locally before every commit (CI's minutes had run out), the server build included.

- **`@ow/collab`** (steps 1a `a4702dd`, 1b `1640b7a`): `SharedDefinition<T>` holds a definition in a Yjs document (`.value` with structural sharing, `.set(next, origin)` writing the difference, `.textAt(path)`, `.delete(path)`); identity and text-path rules per editor in `spec.ts` (`workflowSpec`, `appSpec`); 600 seeded property runs (2 and 3 replicas, random edits from the editors' own functions, random sync orders) converging on schema-valid definitions, which found three merge rules (an object whose `type`/`kind` changes is replaced whole; positions are atomic; strings stored well-formed); `SharedHistory` (one's own undo with M13's grouping and views).
- **Server** (steps 2a `23b9a57`, 2b-1 `c722fdc`, 2b-2 `6b5c97c`): migrations 0021 (`collab_documents`, `collab_updates`) and 0022 (a NOTIFY trigger on every draft revision); `services/collab/` (`target.ts` keys and rights, `store.ts` load/append/compact/write/fold under advisory locks, `rooms.ts` one room per open document per process with a serial queue); `GET /api/collab/<key>` over `@fastify/websocket`; the draft written at most once a second and on the last leave, schema-checked; other writes folded in; updates and presence relayed between API processes through `ow_collab`.
- **Workflow editor** (steps 3a-1 `7105bec`, 3a-2 `0a50513`, 3b `2568b08`): `CollabProvider` (reconnects with backoff, offline edits merged on reconnect, saves on request: `MESSAGE_SAVE`); the store writes through the shared document; the save badge is the connection (Connecting, Saved, Saving, Offline, Not saved); the conflict path is gone; undo/redo are one's own.
- **Code fields** (steps 4a `6357573`, 4b `5e47a41`): our own Monaco binding to `Y.Text` (`components/code/text-binding.ts`) and others' carets and selections in their colour (`remote-cursors.ts`).
- **Presence** (step 5 `2c819e4`, app 6b `9f7e7a2`): avatars in the header, others' selected blocks or components outlined with name tags, live pointers on the canvas and the grid (`lib/presence.ts`, `components/presence-avatars.tsx`).
- **App and module editors** (steps 6a-1 `5fd74f2`, 6a-2 `5877335`): `lib/collab-session.ts` shared by both editors; the app store on the shared document with one's own undo; code fields bound, except the four parsed ones (props, a variable's initial value, a resource query's JSON, library params); cleared optional fields removed in the same step.
- **Access and edge cases** (steps 7a `b78eee0`, 7b `d2b614c`): access re-checked on open sockets every 30 s (`COLLAB_ACCESS_CHECK_MS`), a lost one closing with 4403 and its reason ("Disconnected: reload", read-only); tests for Git branch drafts, watchers with Use, and a 500-block workflow (a keystroke across replicas 1.8 ms median).
- **Done-when** (step 8a `579bfeb`): `apps/web/e2e/m16-done-when.spec.ts`; passed in Playwright and on Docker Compose with each person on a different API process (§10).
- **New dependencies** (MIT): yjs 13.6.33, y-protocols 1.0.7, lib0 0.2.119, @fastify/websocket 11.3.3 (bringing ws). y-monaco was not used.
- **Review fixes** (session 20, the `code-review` skill on `feat/m15-webhooks...feat/m16-multiplayer`: 10 found, 9 M16's, fixed in one commit; the 10th, the library editor's uncommitted `gcTime: 0`, isn't M16's and was left to the user): the server reads a stored document under its lock (a compaction between its two queries lost updates on a resync); a draft folded in on load is announced like any fold (rooms open in other processes missed it, and their next write could undo a REST save); a room opening on a stored document ahead of its draft writes it (a failed write or a process gone left `dirty` behind, so a save request said "Saved" without writing); presence ids are freed on any removal (one owned by a process that went stayed refused after its 30 s timeout); the browser's reconnect backoff resets once synced, not when the socket opens (a server failing after the upgrade was retried every 500 ms); `ensureSaved` gives up after 10 s again ("Saving the draft took too long": Run, publish and branch switches hung offline) and a destroyed provider answers waiting saves; a session the server ended isn't reused when its draft is opened again (the editor stayed "Disconnected"); text fields are stored with `\n` line ends (a CRLF from Git or REST shifted the Monaco binding's offsets); M13's snapshot history, now dead, removed (`lib/history.ts` keeps `changeKey`; the stores use `@ow/collab`'s `equal`). Tests: server `collab.test.ts` +4, `@ow/collab` `provider.test.ts` (new, 2) and a CRLF test, web `collab-session.test.ts` (new, 2); each fails without its fix.
- **Totals:** collab 35, web unit 72 (71; M13's history tests went with its code), server 395 and 1 skipped (368), e2e 63 in 35 files (51 at the end of M15).

## 9l. What M17 built (on the local branch `feat/m17-ssh-tunnels`, not pushed)

The design is `architecture.md` §2.20 (with "As built"); the step record in §14. The plan (`3be1953`) and seven steps, each part green before its commit, plus a fix the Compose check found, stacked on `feat/m16-multiplayer`. Checked locally before every commit.

- **Keys** (step 1a, `5009fc4`): `packages/connectors/src/ssh/keys.ts` `readPrivateKey`: OpenSSH through `ssh2`; PEM and PKCS#8 through `node:crypto`; PuTTY `.ppk` v2 and v3 (Argon2 from Node 24) through our reader; all but OpenSSH written as an unencrypted OpenSSH key in memory; type, fingerprint, public half; `SshKeyError` codes. 23 fixture keys made with `ssh-keygen` and `puttygen`.
- **Settings** (step 1b, `95f5add`): `ssh` in the Postgres, MySQL and REST configs (1–3 hops with stable ids, key or password, a pinned host key) and per-hop secrets keyed by hop id (write-only, masked, scrubbed like the rest); `normalizeSsh` (pins of moved hops dropped unless re-trusted, unused secrets dropped), `sshCredentials` (keys read on save and test, errors naming the server); server `settleSsh`.
- **Tunnels** (steps 2a `467858e`, 2b `f8521c1`): `SshTunnel`: hops connected in order over `forwardOut`, host keys checked before signing in (`seen` keeps what each presented), `open(host, port)` → a forwarded stream (no local port), one SSH connection shared by a pool, keepalives (15 s × 3), reconnect on the next open, `SshError` codes; `sshTunnelStats` and the metrics `ow_ssh_tunnels_open`, `ow_ssh_tunnel_reconnects_total`; `startTestSshServer` in `@ow/connectors/testing`.
- **Drivers** (steps 3a `087a304`, 3b `28eaf00`): Postgres `socket`, MySQL `stream` (`deferredStream`; the KILL QUERY side connection too), REST an undici `connect` (TLS over the channel for https); the client owns its tunnel; SSH failures as `connection` errors with `details.ssh`; a tunnelled REST resource skips the private-network guard, and its client-credentials token goes through the tunnel.
- **Test connection** (step 4, `3d7f373`): `SshTunnel.probe()` hop by hop; `testResource` returns `ssh` steps; `sshKeys` in `resources.get`/`saveConfig`. A bug found: postgres only ends a socket whose `readyState` is `'open'`, so tunnelled clients took 5 s to close (`socketLike`).
- **Web** (steps 5a `615d073`, 5b `2945cce`): `pages/resources/ssh-tunnel.tsx`: the SSH section (hops, keys or passwords, key details with Copy public key, the trusted host key) and Test connection's steps with Trust (and a red warning for a changed key).
- **Ops** (step 6, `fe79bce`): `onSshTunnelEvent` and `services/ssh-log.ts` (connected, connected again, dropped, failed; once per process from `main.ts`); `docs/runbooks/ssh-tunnels.md`.
- **Done-when** (step 7a, `fc35ec1`): `apps/web/e2e/m17-done-when.spec.ts`; passed in Playwright and on Docker Compose against real OpenSSH (§10).
- **Fix** (`9840ec7`, found by the Compose check): a server behind a jump host going away only ends its channel (OpenSSH closes after an EOF back; ssh2 emits `end` and stops its keepalives), so the tunnel never noticed; a hop's `end` is now a drop, and the test SSH server behaves like sshd.
- **Review fixes** (session 20, the `code-review` skill on `feat/m16-multiplayer...feat/m17-ssh-tunnels`: 10 found; the 10th is the uncommitted library-editor `gcTime: 0` again, not M17's): Postgres `verify-full` through a tunnel checked the certificate against "localhost" (postgres takes TLS's `servername` from `socket.host`; channels now carry `host` and `port`); Test connection hid a forward refused after every hop worked (the generic note now hides only for a failed step); a second `error` before a hop's `ready` could be unhandled (`on` with a guard); the probe could run past Test connection's 15 s (a `deadline` for the chain); the server dropped a pin re-trusted for a moved hop (pins now kept as sent: what's trusted is the key; the form clears them on an edit); a `.ppk`'s Argon2 cost is bounded (256 MiB, 256 passes, parallelism 16; missing values refused) and its failures are `unreadable`, not a 500; `resources.get` remembers each key's public details (by a hash of key and passphrase, 100 at most) instead of re-running Argon2; MySQL's connect timeout leaves the SSH servers their 15 s each. Not changed: Test connection connects the chain twice (probe, then the client's own tunnel: a test-only cost, the probe kept apart from the client); authorization-code OAuth stays outside the tunnel (a known gap; the runbook now says so plainly). Tests: connectors +5 (a fake TLS Postgres behind the tunnel, checked to fail without the fix; channel `host`/`port`; the probe's deadline; MySQL's timeout; Argon2 bounds), the server pin test rewritten, e2e `ssh-tunnel.spec.ts` +1 step (checked to fail with the old condition).
- **New dependencies** (MIT): ssh2 1.17.0 (in `@ow/connectors` and declared in `apps/server` for the bundle), @types/ssh2 1.15.6 (dev). Its optional native cpu-features isn't built.
- **Totals:** connectors 205 (147 at the end of M16), server 403 and 1 skipped (395), web unit 72, e2e 66 in 37 files (63).

## 9m. What M18 built (on the local branch `feat/m18-iap`, not pushed)

The design is `architecture.md` §2.21 (with "As built"); the step record in §14. The plan (`4493f56`) and six steps, each part green before its commit, plus two fixes found on the way, stacked on `feat/m17-ssh-tunnels`. Checked locally before every commit.

- **M17's follow-up** (step 1, `3cb36d2`): a refused `localhost`/`127.0.0.1`/`::1` SSH host hints at `host.docker.internal`.
- **Google identities** (steps 2a `59b2782`, 2b `03c8888`): `packages/connectors/src/google-auth.ts`: BigQuery's service account tokens moved there (cached, refreshed a minute early); `serverIdentity()`: `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` (`service_account` or `authorized_user`), gcloud's application-default file, then the metadata server; `describe()` says whose.
- **The IAP stream** (steps 3a `3b22550`, 3b `95fd278`): `ssh/iap.ts` `openIapStream`: IAP's WebSocket subprotocol (16 KiB data frames, acks), close codes to `iap_auth`/`iap_target`/`iap_connect` with hints, resume after a drop or 4004 (unacked bytes resent, up to 30 s); a fake IAP endpoint in `ssh/testing.ts`.
- **Tunnels through IAP** (step 4a, `980f91c`): a hop's `reach: 'iap'` and `iap {project, zone, instance, interface, identity}` (first hop only), the `iapKey` secret, `SshTunnel` opening hop 0 over IAP, the IAP step in `probe()`, `configureIap`.
- **Server** (steps 4b-1 `771eae1`, 4b-2 `c93acc3`, 4b-3 `30946b1`): `GOOGLE_SERVER_IDENTITY` (`allow`/`deny`) and test URLs; `services/iap.ts` (`configureGoogleIap` at startup in the API and the worker; `resources.serverIdentity` for the form); `checkSshIap` on save and test; `iapKeys` (a key's `client_email`); the admin rule on the server-identity target, on save and test, with the audit's `serverIdentityTarget`; `ow_iap_resumes_total`; Test connection end to end through the fake.
- **Web** (steps 5a `f33bf7a`, 5b `7ba1260`): `pages/resources/ssh-tunnel.tsx`: "Reach it", the VM's fields, the identity (a key showing its account, or the server's, admins only, locked for editors), Test connection's IAP line.
- **Done-when** (step 6a, `5c7ddfe`): `apps/web/e2e/m18-done-when.spec.ts` with control routes on global setup's fake IAP; passed in Playwright and on Docker Compose against the real bastion (§10).
- **Fixes found on the way:** `ws` undeclared in `apps/server` (`cb21e1e`: the bundled server and the image couldn't start since 3a; unit tests run from source); M17's SSH server box ~3000 px wide once a key was stored (`4cbbf92`).
- **Ops** (step 6c): `docker-compose.gcloud.yml` (opts in, mounts the host's gcloud application-default file read-only); the runbook's "Through Google Cloud IAP" (`docs/runbooks/ssh-tunnels.md`), its codes and metric; `.env.example`.
- **Review fixes** (session 22, the `code-review` skill on `feat/m17-ssh-tunnels...feat/m18-iap`: 10 found, all M18's, all fixed). The IAP stream (`ssh/iap.ts`): the first connect never took a fresh token after the handshake refused a cached one (now once, on HTTP 401/403 only: `IapError.tokenRefused`; not after a 4033, where a new token has the same roles); a clean close (1000) left waiting writes hanging forever (`closedByPeer`: they fail, the stream ends and is destroyed); a frame cut short threw a RangeError out of the `ws` listener, an uncaught exception that could take down the process (lengths checked, `iap_connect` "malformed frame"); ending our side during a resume kept resuming and left an open session (stops, or closes once resumed); `sendAck` with no WebSocket recorded an ack it never sent (now sent once the WebSocket is set). The form: "Add a jump host" put the new hop before a first server reached through IAP, leaving a config that couldn't be saved or fixed (now after it); another project or zone kept the old VM's trusted host key (a false "host key changed"). Smaller: `probeIap` waited for `describe()` before opening IAP, counted in its time (now alongside); the metadata server's email asked on every `serverIdentity` query (remembered once found); the IAP scope defined twice (`IAP_SCOPE` exported from connectors). Tests: 4 in `ssh-iap.test.ts` (each failed on the code before), a third test in `iap.spec.ts` (failed before at the zone step).
- **New dependencies** (MIT): ws 8.22.0 (in `@ow/connectors` and declared in `apps/server` for the bundle), @types/ws 8.18.2 (dev).
- **Totals:** connectors 237 (205 at the end of M17), server 413 and 1 skipped (403), web unit 72, e2e 70 in 39 files (66).

### M18 step record (was §14 during M18)

M18 is planned (`architecture.md` §2.7 row, §2.21). The user chose (session 21): IAP to **a VM by name**, with **a service account key or the server's own identity**. It came out of connecting Optiply's read-only production resource that day: the API runs in a container, so the SSH host `localhost:2222` (a `gcloud start-iap-tunnel` on the Mac) was refused; `host.docker.internal:2222` worked, but only while that command runs.

The design is `architecture.md` §2.22 (with "As built"); the step record in §14. The plan (`0b9ce7e`) and six steps, each part green before its commit, stacked on `feat/m18-iap`. Checked locally before every commit.

- **The Pub/Sub resource** (steps 1a `004232e`, 1b `bf3f547`): `packages/connectors/src/pubsub/`: a REST client (pull, acknowledge, modifyAckDeadline, nack, publish, get, list topics; errors naming the roles; a 401 retried with a fresh token), a fake Pub/Sub endpoint (deadlines, redelivery, nack, dead letters, ordering keys, failures; exported from `@ow/connectors/testing`), the `pubsub` connector (a key or the server's identity, Test connection, the publish query) and `configurePubSub`.
- **Definition, database, releases** (step 2a `03a309a`): `triggers.pubsub` and its validation; migration `0023` (`pubsub` kinds, trigger leases, `pubsub_deliveries`); projection like schedules; releasing needs Use on a trigger's resource; `@ow/collab` matches the triggers by name.
- **Server** (step 2b `f5f507d`): `services/google.ts` `configureGoogle` (IAP and Pub/Sub); M18's admin rule for a Pub/Sub resource on the server's identity; settings checked on save.
- **The consumer** (steps 3a `3b29f69`, 3b `865b0e6`): `packages/engine/src/pubsub-consumer.ts`: a lease per trigger, a pull loop within `maxInFlight`, a run per delivery (`startTrigger.data`, `startTrigger.message`), duplicates from `pubsub_deliveries`, a hold loop (deadlines extended, ack on success, nack on failure or cancel, released past `maxHoldMinutes`), nacks on stop; started in every worker; metrics `ow_pubsub_*`; retention of delivery records (32 days).

- **Web** (steps 5a `afcc089`, 5b `ebdfa0a`): the resource's settings and badge, publish queries (workflow inspector, playground), the triggers form's Pub/Sub section with each trigger's live line, runs showing their message, `startTrigger.message` typed. **A bug found:** the triggers form's `updateTriggers` kept only webhook and schedules.
- **Done-when** (step 6a `08c4cde`): `apps/web/e2e/m19-done-when.spec.ts`; passed in Playwright and on Docker Compose against real Pub/Sub (§10).
- **Ops** (step 6c): `docs/runbooks/pubsub.md`; `.env.example` and `docker-compose.gcloud.yml` mention Pub/Sub.

The consumer: a message whose run **succeeded but wasn't recorded** (its worker stopped in the second before the hold check) **was run again** on redelivery: `startDelivery` now records an ended run's outcome and acks one that succeeded; a failed pull dropped the client while the hold loop still used it (acks and extensions failed): they get it through `pubsub()`, the old client closed 30 s later; a new release restarted every subscriber and nacked everything held: the release id is swapped in when the settings didn't change; acks, nacks and extensions had no timeout (a hung call stalled holding): 15 s; `finish()` answered a stale ack id when a redelivery had replaced the entry; state written only when it changed; **a worker that stopped pulling a trigger kept its lease until it lapsed** (found by the done-when after the fixes: the editor showed a turned-off trigger "pulling" up to 30 s): it gives it up at once. The client: **the server's identity could reach any project** by a full topic or subscription name, past the admin-approved project: `ownProjectOnly` refuses others (a key's own roles still decide for keys). Releases: a trigger in an environment where its resource has no settings went live and failed every pull: refused. The editor: problems for "At once", "Hold up to" and the environment are shown on the card, the numbers clamped to their limits. Tests: consumer +3 (each failing on the code before), a lease assertion, connectors +1 assertion, releases +1; existing release tests configure their resource.

     **Done (session 22):** `packages/engine/src/pubsub-consumer.ts` `PubSubConsumer({db, resources, workerId, log?, intervalMs 5 s, leaseMs 30 s, retryMs 1 s})`: `tick()` takes or renews leases in one `update … returning` (ours, nobody's, or lapsed), stops pullers for triggers lost, disabled or dropped, and restarts one whose release, environment or settings changed; `stop()` stops pulling and gives the leases up; `stats` (triggers, in flight). A `Subscriber` per trigger pulls up to `maxInFlight − held` (90 s per pull; errors logged as `pubsub pull failed`, retried from `retryMs` doubling to 30 s, with the resource's client made again so fixed settings are picked up). `startDelivery(db, trigger, received)` under the delivery row's lock: `done` (succeeded or released: acked), `running` (the latest run still queued/running/waiting: held for it), or `started` (a run with key `pubsub:<trigger>:<message>:<n>`, `triggerType: 'pubsub'`, the delivery row updated). **The run's input:** `{data, message}`: `startTrigger.data` is the message's data (parsed JSON, else text) and **`startTrigger.message`** `{messageId, publishTime, attributes, orderingKey?, deliveryAttempt?, subscription, dataBase64}` (engine `RunInput.message`, `PubSubRunMessage`; the view adds `message` only when there is one), not the flat `{data, attributes, …}` §2.22 said. One worker takes every free trigger (no spreading between workers yet). **Not started in the worker yet** (3b, once it acks and nacks). `test/pubsub-consumer.test.ts` (7; 3 runs green). Engine 156.
   - 3b: holding (deadline extensions while the run lasts, `maxHoldMinutes`), ack on success, nack on failure or cancel (the run's end heard by LISTEN, with a poll as fallback), a consumer gone mid-run (another takes over without a second success); triggers disabled or dropped by a release stop; metrics and logs; tests.
     **Done (session 22):** each `Subscriber` has a hold loop beside its pull loop: every `checkMs` (1 s) while it holds messages, one query for their runs' statuses: `succeeded` → recorded, then **acked**; `failed`, `cancelled` or gone → recorded, then **nacked** (redelivered by Pub/Sub: its backoff and dead-letter policy); held past `maxHoldMinutes` → `released`, acked, the run goes on (warned); the rest extended to `holdDeadlineSeconds` (60) every `extendEveryMs` (20 s), and at once when first held (a subscription's own deadline is short). The outcome is recorded before the ack, so a lost ack is recognised on redelivery. **Polled, not LISTENed** (one query per trigger per second, only while holding; simpler than sharing the dispatcher's listener). `stop()` nacks what it holds, so the next worker gets those messages at once and holds them for their runs. `pubsubStats` (`inFlight`, `acked`, `nacked`, `duplicate`, `released`, `pullErrors`) → metrics `ow_pubsub_messages_total{outcome}`, `ow_pubsub_in_flight`, `ow_pubsub_pull_errors_total`. Started in `worker/index.ts` under the dispatcher's worker id, stopped first. Retention prunes finished `pubsub_deliveries` after `PUBSUB_DELIVERY_DAYS` (32: Pub/Sub keeps an unacked message 31 days at most), never one still running. Tests: consumer +4 (11, 5 runs green: ack/nack/cancel and redelivery; no redelivery while a long run is held; released past the hold; handed over on stop), retention +1, metrics. Engine 161, server 419.

## 9o. What M20 built (on the local branch `feat/m20-merge`, not pushed)

The design is `architecture.md` §2.23 (with "As built"); the step record in §14. The plan (`267b184`) and five steps, each part green before its commit, stacked on `feat/m19-pubsub` (whose tip is now the library-editor fix `5163f75`). Checked locally before every commit.

- **The merge** (steps 1a `61aa531`, 1b `254f74f`): `packages/collab/src/merge.ts`: `merge(spec, base, ours, theirs, resolutions?)` and `mergeObject` (name, description, the definition; a schema `check` turning misfits into whole-item conflicts), `mergeText`/`composeText` on `node-diff3`; `libraryQuerySpec`; the convergence tests' generators shared (`test/generate.ts`) and property tests over them.
- **Update from branch** (steps 2a `d681c19`, 2b `33c4dc4`): `services/git/branch-drafts.ts` (`updateDraftTo`, `mergeIntoDraft`, the outlook in `branchDraftChanges`), `kinds.ts` `mergeKindObject`, the router's `resolutions`.
- **Update from main** (steps 3a `e284441`, 3b `a1a8e71`): `GitRepo.mergeBase`/`isAncestor`/`mergeTree`/merge commits; `services/git/update-from-main.ts`; `branchDrafts.updateFromMain`; `mainAhead`; audit `git.merged_main`.
- **Web** (steps 4a `a6a9f72`, 4b `36cc5bc`): `lib/merge-choices.ts`, `components/merge-dialog.tsx`, the Git bar (Update from branch / from main, "Update, then commit", "<main> changed this <kind>"). **A bug found:** the shared `Dialog` closed on an Escape pressed in a Monaco field.
- **Done-when** (step 5a `e86fd23`): `apps/web/e2e/m20-done-when.spec.ts` (+ `git-merge.spec.ts`, `git-helpers.ts`); passed in Playwright and, by hand, against a real GitHub repo (§10).
- **Review fixes** (session 23, the `code-review` skill on `feat/m19-pubsub...feat/m20-merge`: 9 found, all M20's, all fixed in one commit). **The merge base drifted** for a draft whose base commit had no files for the object (created protected, or opened before its protect PR merged): it was the object's live release or draft *at merge time*, so a deploy in between could silently revert a change: migration `0025` adds `git_branch_drafts.base_value` (what the draft started from), set at open, used as the base, cleared once a commit or an update gives the draft files (older drafts fall back as before). **A draft update failing after Update from main's push** escaped (no audit, an error for a merge that happened): it comes back as `merged` with `draft: null` and `draftError`, the bar saying to use Update from branch. **A failed save before merging** was an unhandled rejection and left "Update, then commit" armed: shown, and reset (also on the mutations' errors). The changes query's outlook and `mainAhead` are **cached** by commits and revision (500 entries) instead of recomputed every 30 s per editor. The merge dialog showed the other update's stale error and the bar hid other errors while it was open: each shows its own, the updates reset when starting. A dead loop in `items()` removed; `isRecord` shared from `shared.ts`; `objectDir` instead of a hand-built path; `reloadDraft` shared; the router's doc comment back above it. Tests: +2 server (the drift case fails without the fix; the post-push failure audited), +0 elsewhere; full server 438 and 1 skipped; collab 64 (property tests at 5,000 seeds); the Git e2e specs 16 passed.
- **Before M20, on `feat/m19-pubsub`:** `5163f75`, the library editor's `gcTime: 0` (a deleted-then-recreated library query opened as the old one), committed on the user's word with a spec that fails without it.

- **Modules inside modules** (steps 4a-1 `1f57008`, 4a-2 `1f328cd`, 4a-3 `efdfa41`, 4b `3e49942`, 4c `d6ea47c`): `module_depth`/`module_cycle`, `ModuleInfo.nesting`, `moduleNesting`; `services/module-nesting.ts` (`loadNesting`, `checkLiveNesting`); `moduleReleaseTree`, `walkInstancePath`; the runtime starting nested instances, paths as `InstanceStep[]`, `AppScope` composing prefixes; the module editor placing modules (the picker leaving out what would be refused). **A bug found:** the module editor never ran `useModuleSync`.
- **The library query editor multiplayer** (steps 5a `bf9e722`, 5b `351a758`): the `library:<id>` key, migration **0026** (`collab_documents.library_query_id`, the trigger on `library_queries`), `pages/library/store.ts` and `collab.ts`.

- **Done-when** (step 7a `b00ae2b`): `apps/web/e2e/m21-done-when.spec.ts`; passed in Playwright and, by hand, on the user's Compose stack (§10).
- **Review fixes** (session 24, the `code-review` skill on `feat/m20-merge...feat/m21-reuse`: 10 found, all M21's, all fixed in one commit, not committed yet): **a live release's manual run was refused** after its library query's live release dropped a parameter it passes (`checkDefinition`'s new `checkLibraries`: off for live runs, which were checked at publishing); the library editor's REST JSON text was stale (set once before the store loaded; it now follows the query when it changes some other way); the module panel's Used by ignored modules holding the module; renaming an input to another input's name merged their reads, and two inputs sharing a name were both replaced (`updateModuleItem` replaces the first only and rewrites reads only when unambiguous); an invalid input name left the reads behind for good (the module panel refuses invalid or taken names, saying why); reaching into an instance on a page not opened yet threw, and an edit dropped started instances on other pages (`route` starts it on demand; `startInstances` keeps those running before); the draft's release tree required Use at every level (now the outermost only, as the server runs them); `moduleInfos` loaded every module release in the database (`loadNesting`'s `roots`: only those reachable); a library runner looked up the release and user per item (once per attempt now); `InstanceStep` defined four times (one interface in `@ow/app-definition`, one zod schema `instanceStepSchema`). Tests: `library-workflows.test.ts` +1 (fails without the fix), `nested-modules.test.ts` +1, `app-runtime` `modules.test.ts` +2 (both fail without the fix), `module.test.ts` +1, engine `library.test.ts` (the release resolved once for a loop); e2e `modules.spec.ts`'s empty Used by text.
- **New dependencies:** none. **Migration:** `0026_collab_library_queries`.

## 9q. What M22 built (on the local branch `feat/m22-git-gaps`, not pushed)

The design is `architecture.md` §2.25 (with "As built"); the step record below. The plan (`e4b66a4`) and six steps, most split into parts, each part green before its commit, stacked on `feat/m21-reuse`. Checked locally before every commit.

- **The format** (steps 1a `a352523`, 1b `c69aa04`): `@ow/git-format` `settings.ts` (`resources/<slug>/resource.json`, `config-vars/<NAME>/config-var.json`, `SettingsKind`, codecs, `splitRepo`'s `settings`), `meta.ts` `folder` (`folderPathSchema`) on every object with meta.
- **Resources and config vars as Git objects** (steps 2a-1 `5a38e45`, 2a-2 `dacaceb`, 2b `2544560`): migration **0027** (`git_protected` on both, `resource_configs.overrides`, `config_var_values.local`, the kinds in `git_objects`), `services/git/settings-objects.ts` (protect/unprotect, `settingsChanges`, `commitSettings`), the effective config (`applyOverrides`, `effectiveConfig`, read by `toResolved`, OAuth and the release check), `resources.setOverrides`, read-only rules on main.
- **Deploying them** (steps 3a-1 `f9c27b4`, 3a-2 `7cb5da5`, 3a-3a `d50fbe5`, 3a-3b `42762bf`): `services/git/deploy-settings.ts` (`deployConfigVar`, `deployResource`: created protected without an owner, "needs secrets", secrets/overrides/local values kept, a type change refused), the order (config vars, resources, then the rest), `services/git/folders.ts` (deploys move objects into their Git folder), migration **0028** (`git_branch_drafts.folder`).
- **Retries, health, notify, Promote** (steps 3b-1 `4289e23`, 3b-2 `087f6a9`, 3b-3 `4086424`): migration **0029** (`git_settings.retry_requested_at`, trigger `retry`, `GIT_RETRY_MINUTES`), migration **0030** (`notify_workflow_id`, `notified_problems`, run trigger type `git`), `services/git/health.ts` (`currentProblems`, `gitHealth`, `notifyIfChanged`), `git.openPromotion`.
- **The web** (steps 4a-1 `56b9dfb`, 4a-2 `b2415e6`, 4a-3 `9b0049a`, 4b `0ae77ae`): protect and In Git on resources and config vars, the Save to branch dialog (`components/settings-git.tsx`), the overrides card, "Save here only", `components/git-health.tsx` (banner, badges), Settings → Git's Notify and Promote cards.
- **Editors** (steps 5a-1 `bebae86`, 5a-2 `92c5386`, 5a-4 `959dcf5`, 5b-1 `d8c7554`, 5b-2 `489a96f`): `git.branches({kind, id})` and the grouped picker, the Details dialog (`branchDrafts.setDetails`), the app editor read-only on main, `@ow/collab` `renames.ts` (`Renamer`, `findRenames`, `workflowRenamer`, `appRenamer`; `mergeObject`'s `renamer`) passed per kind by the server's `mergeKindObject`, the merge dialog's rename note.
- **Done-when** (steps 6a-1 `532cf01`, 6a-2 `aa4e9fd`): `apps/web/e2e/instances.ts` (a spec's own repo, fake GitHub and servers) and `m22-done-when.spec.ts`; passed in Playwright and, by hand, on the user's Compose stack (§10). Also `5703c84` (no background git in the e2e's temporary clones: an ENOTEMPTY flake).
- **Review fixes** (session 27, the `code-review` skill on `feat/m21-reuse...feat/m22-git-gaps`: 10 found plus the `git_objects` row, all M22's, all fixed in one commit): **an override path could pollute `Object.prototype`** (`__proto__.x` through `resources.setOverrides`, before validation; now `isOverridePath` refuses prototype keys and `applyOverrides` walks own properties only); **objects protected before M22 lost their folder** on their next deploy (`folder` is now three-state in the format: absent = a pre-M22 file, left where it is; `null` = the top level, always written by M22; a path; a branch draft opened from an old file starts from the object's folder); **a deploy deleted an environment's config with its secrets** when Git's file didn't list the environment (kept now when it holds secrets, overrides or OAuth accounts: `holdsLocalSettings`); a cleared local config var value waited for the next commit (a retry now also runs for deployed directories marked to deploy again); the timed retry fetched and deployed new commits with polling off (`retryOnly` re-checks the deployed commit without reading the branch); folder names may no longer hold `/`, and writing files for an object in an older such folder is refused (`gitFolderPath`); Test connection on a protected resource ignored this instance's overrides; folders holding protected objects (below them too) can't be renamed or deleted (`holdsProtected`); the branch picker's heads are read at most every 15 s per process (at once after a push from here) and each branch's answer is kept by its commits; `findRenames` renames each gone item once to a probe name and compares JSON for the pairs (the probe written as the new name), so a merge does one whole-definition rename per gone item, not one per pair (a first try that blanked the name as a word missed a block named like the table in its SQL: the M22 done-when caught it); migration **0031** `git_objects_cleanup` (a trigger per object table deletes an object's deploy row; old orphans removed) and clearing Git settings empties `git_objects`.
- **New dependencies:** none. **Migrations:** `0027_git_settings_objects`, `0028_branch_draft_folder`, `0029_git_deploy_retries`, `0030_git_health_notify`, `0031_git_objects_cleanup` (review).
- **Totals:** git-format 2435, collab 70, definition 177 (as at the end of M21), app-definition 96 (95), engine 171 (171), app-runtime 35 (33), db 20, server 505 and 1 skipped (456), web unit 79 (78), e2e 92 in 54 files (83 in 47).

M22 is planned (`architecture.md` §2.7 row, §2.25). The user chose (session 25): of the remaining milestones, **Git sync's gaps**; resources and config vars **protected and committed like workflows** (protect opens a PR; on main their settings are read-only; the form's Save to branch commits and opens a PR; secrets never in Git); **Git plus local overrides** between instances (Git holds each environment's settings; an instance may override single non-secret fields; promotion is a PR between deploy branches); deploy problems shown as **an admin banner plus a workflow to run** (named in Settings → Git); and all four small gaps: name, description and folder on branches (with read-only inspectors on main), following renames in a merge, folders in Git, the branch list per object. Deploys retry on their own.

Committed 5a-4 as `959dcf5` (5a done). **5b-1 done (session 26, not committed yet):** `packages/collab/src/renames.ts` (new): `Renamer<D>` (`items`: named items by collection; `rename`: with references rewritten, null when it can't), `findRenames` (an item gone and one new item in the same collection equal to it once the base has the rename; renamed-and-changed on one side isn't recognised), `workflowRenamer` (main-graph blocks, functions: `renameBlock`, `renameFunction`), `appRenamer` (components, queries, transformers, variables: `renameAppEntity`; pages: `renamePage`). `merge.ts` `mergeObject(…, {renamer})`: `followRenames` first applies each side's renames to the base and the other side (only where the other side still has the old name and not the new one: a rename against a delete stays as before), then the usual merge; the same rename on both sides is taken; different ones are a **value conflict at `['definition', <collection>, <old>, 'name']`** (ours/theirs pick the name; left at ours), so the dialog labels it like "Block fetch · name". ## 9r. What M23 built (on the local branch `feat/m23-python-followups`, not pushed)

The design is `architecture.md` §2.26 (with "As built"); the step record below. The plan (`81a256e`) and six steps, the big ones split into parts, each green before its commit, stacked on `feat/m22-git-gaps`. Checked locally before every commit.

- **Logs as they print** (steps 1a `56f4a49`, 1b `87a67fe`): the executor's `/run` with `stream: true` answers NDJSON (a line each as printed, a partial line after 0.25 s without cutting a character, the outcome last); `PythonExecutorClient.run` reads it (`readStream`), so the lines reach the run's log while the block runs.
- **The warm pool** (steps 2a `91dbf32`, 2b `1d6d77c`): `Jail` and `Pool` in `executor/server.py` (`PYTHON_WARM_JAILS`, `PYTHON_PREIMPORT`, `PYTHON_WARM_MAX_IDLE_SECONDS`, `PYTHON_WARM_START_PARALLEL`), `runner.py`'s `warm_up` and `limit_cpu`, `os._exit` after the outcome; `warm` in the outcome, the log and `/health`'s `pool`; `ow_python_executions_total{jail}`; the runbook's "The warm pool".
- **Packages in the definition** (step 3 `1d1872b`): `@ow/definition` `requirements.ts` (`parseRequirement`, `requirementProblems`, `PythonLock`), `settings.python.requirements`, the validator's `invalid_requirement`; `@ow/git-format`'s `requirements.txt`; the Settings field (and `withSetting`: clearing the run timeout used to drop every setting).
- **The builder** (steps 4a `746e765`, 4b `ea93da2`): `executor/builder.py` (`/build`, `/install`, `/health`, the lock, envs on a volume, cleaning), the Dockerfile's `builder` target (pip 26.2.1 by hash); the executor's `envId` (a cold jail, the env bind-mounted read-only, first on `sys.path`; `python_packages` when missing); test helpers `startTestIndex` (wheels made in the test), `startTestBuilder`, `testEnvsVolume`.
- **The server** (steps 5a-1 `f569d98`, 5a-2 `2913086`, 5b `27191d9`): migration **0032** (`python_envs`, `workflow_releases.python_lock`), `services/python-envs.ts` (`PythonBuilderClient`, `envForRequirements`, `releaseLock`, `dbPythonEnvs`), the engine's `pythonEnv` (`PythonEnvProvider`, `shared`, `waitForEnv`), `python_packages_unavailable`, the lock at publish, rollback and Git deploys, audit `python.env_built`, Compose's `python-builder` (volume `python-envs`, network `builder-egress`), `workflows.pythonEnv` and `workflows.installPythonEnv`, the editor's install state.
- **Done-when** (step 6a `075b3e5`): `apps/web/e2e/m23-done-when.spec.ts`; passed in Playwright and, by hand, on the user's Compose stack (§10).
- **Review fixes** (session 28, the `code-review` skill on `feat/m22-git-gaps...feat/m23-python-followups`: 10 found, all M23's, all fixed in one commit): **runs queued behind builds** (a cached `/install` took a build slot; now answered without one), **a queued build lost its time** (counted from the slot now), **a rollback after a platform change resolved the loose requirements** (one `installLock` pins the lock's versions), **a failing package set built twice per run** (`prefetch`), **warm jails had ~45 MB less memory** (their limit is raised by the imports), **a stalled stream reader held a run past its deadline** (writes time out after 10 s), the cleaning's race with an install, the size walked per run, and two editor ones (the install's answer under the wrong requirements; the field deaf to others' changes). Details in the step record below.
- **New dependencies:** none in the app; the builder image's pip 26.2.1 (by hash). **Migrations:** `0032_python_envs`.
- **Totals** (after the review): executor package 47 (24 at the end of M22; now executor 36, builder 7, packages 4), sandbox 28 + 1 skipped (24), definition 181 (177), git-format 2439 (2435), engine 176 (171), db 20, server 522 + 1 skipped (513), web unit 79, e2e 96 (92).

**Progress:** step 0 written (`ae01b19`); 1a `178a60f`, 1b `997a163`, 2a `83071b7` (the kind's Git wiring came with it: the compiler needed it). Git deploy of a library is refused until 3b (`deploy.ts` throws). Decided while building: `package-lock.json` isn't written to Git by the platform (releases keep their locks; a deploy starts from the live release's lock); allowed origins may change in the draft by anyone with Edit, and **publishing a release whose origins differ from the live one's needs an admin** (covers collab edits too). 3a `b6e4d54` (2b folded into 2a and 3b), 3b-1 `cdc4596` (build, cache, bundle route), 3b-2 `12aec26` (publish, setLive, uploadBuild, publishImpact, Git deploy from the live lock, unused bundles in retention), 4a `e6a1023` (SDK, frame runtime, CSP document), 4b `996fff5` (runtime, host, renderer, apps.open; e2e `custom-components.spec.ts`), 5 `c9b9b0d` (5a and 5b in one: list, editor, files in Monaco with TypeScript, components panel, dependencies, origins, Build and Preview, releases with Compare; e2e `component-library-editor.spec.ts`). 6 `eeec7da` (Add a custom component, the inspector's Library/Component/Version, resolving releases for the validator and the running draft, a cover over frames in the editor; modules too). 7a `19f5329` (`m25-done-when.spec.ts`, 4 tests); the full e2e 121 passed; review (code-review high: 10 found, all fixed, `221dcc6`); 7b: the image `optiply-workflows:m25-candidate` built and checked by hand (npm 11.19.1 installs a public package as `node`, the server's esbuild bundles it); 7c: docs (this commit). **M25 is built.** Left for the user: rebuild their Compose stack on it (ask first) and try a library with a public npm package there.

- **Review fixes** (session 32, the `code-review` skill (high) on `feat/m25-custom-components...feat/m26-queue-triggers`: 10 found, each checked against the code, all held, all fixed in `a6b2214`): two security ones, **MSK IAM's brokers** (an editor could point an admin's server-credential resource at any host and receive the signed token: the brokers are now part of the admin-only target) and **SQS queue URLs** (a URL's own host got the signed requests, getting around the admin-only endpoint: the SDK's `useQueueUrlAsEndpoint` is off and other hosts are refused); Kafka: a message whose run couldn't start stayed stuck (`undelivered`), a replaced consumer's late revoke wiped the new one's partitions (generations), a topic made again could have messages skipped (ids carry the timestamp), a dead letter sent later went unrecorded (`settled`); MSK IAM's expiry in whole seconds; SQS's visibility batches all sent; the web card chose fields by keys (crashed when a queue was cleared) and kept stale topics text.

- **New dependencies:** `@aws-sdk/client-sqs`, `@aws-sdk/client-sts`, `@aws-sdk/credential-providers` 3.1152.0, `@smithy/signature-v4` 5.7.5, `@confluentinc/kafka-javascript` 1.11.0 (native; its install script downloads a prebuilt binary from GitHub); dev: `@smithy/types`, `aws-msk-iam-sasl-signer-js` 1.0.3 (a test's reference only). **Migrations:** 0034.
